# Ecosystem failure triage

User direction, November 7, 2026: monitor ecosystem CI, fix failures with Stacks and Home first, and push verified fixes as Chris directly to `origin/main`. A 15-minute chat heartbeat continues this work. Notify on meaningful changes or required user input, without repeating unchanged failures.

## Current evidence

- [Engine push cc936893](https://github.com/zig-utils/zig-js/actions/runs/42423315413) passed discovery and the exact-engine/Home runtime build and integration smokes. Its original matrix grouped `home-lang/home` with `home-lang/home-os` in consumer job 12, and `stacksjs/stacks` with `stacksjs/stamped` in job 58. Both jobs were queued when triage began.

These are separate failures. That initial manual run was cancelled after bootstrap verification; automatic push runs remain active.

Existing Home smokes plus new cache, nested/escaped binding, URL-escaping and CommonJS-arguments witnesses pass on system JSC and the local zig-js archive. Local builds used Zig 0.17.0-dev.1857; promotion still needs the exact 0.18 CI bootstrap against the pushed engine.

- **Actual Home harness failure:** [consumer job 128559915415](https://github.com/zig-utils/zig-js-ecosystem/actions/runs/42424124733/job/128559915415) exhausted runner disk in the reference lane and replaced prior result details with an orchestrator exception. - Bun/Node test schedulers and Bun's native build host are unsupported in the current Home tool. Their upstream tests remain unexecuted and red. Never replace them with fake passes or a native-Bun candidate fallback.
- **New cross-runner crash isolated:** in run `42427975707`, the Home consumer's direct build/smokes passed, but the downloaded host crashed during `verify Home.engine` with `Illegal instruction at address 0x387338b` and then timed out. Disassembly of that exact runtime artifact locates address `0x387338b` in `memcpy`: `vmovups (%rsi), %zmm0`, an AVX-512 instruction. The artifact was compiled for the producer runner's native CPU and sent to a different CPU. Shared engine/host builds and their smokes now explicitly use `-Dcpu=baseline`; provenance records the baseline target and publication disassembles `memcpy` to reject this exact ZMM regression. All 22 orchestration tests and actionlint pass; the new portable runtime still needs its CI rebuild before promotion.
- **New Stacks setup evidence:** the completed `42427975707` report preserves all 99 scopes and exposes isolated nested installs failing on `workspace:*` references in cloud, docs, ORM, server, the STX component package and the router-layer deployment. These manifests are not independent registry-only apps. A local Bun/Node fixture reproduces the nested-install error and succeeds when the dependency is actually installed in its ancestor workspace context. A workspace declaration alone is insufficient under isolated linking: an unreferenced member was not visible to Node. Do not blindly skip the nested installs; verify ancestor module availability and compatible versions, preserve all build/test obligations, and retain failures for dependencies that are genuinely unavailable.
- **Workspace setup fix validated locally:** setup now audits installed ancestor providers for workspace-dependent nested apps before attempting a redundant standalone install. Every dependency/dev/peer/optional range must be satisfied, workspace references must resolve to actual declared members, providers must stay inside the checkout, and a child lockfile forces its own install. The setup log retains all provider/version evidence. Unmet requirements still attempt the real install and retain its failure; all 99 Stacks execution scopes remain. Bun 1.4.4 passes 7 real installation tests (26 assertions), including the original nested failure, successful dependency execution after ancestor installation, invisible isolated members, incompatible versions, registry impersonation, excluded members, outside symlinks, child locks, and disabled lifecycle hooks. The six affected local Stacks manifests all pass the provider audit; this is local setup evidence, not a consumer compatibility claim. The 22 Python tests and actionlint also pass. CI validation now runs the Bun setup tests separately from candidate execution.

### 2026-11-12 — real callback filesystem mutations

- [Home 325bb0065](https://github.com/home-lang/home/commit/325bb00651dfb395c397016f60db086f0826066a) implements actual deferred `writeFile`, `mkdir`, `unlink` and `copyFile`, with genuine sync/promise unlink/copy aliases. The owner task queue performs kernel I/O; no libuv worker-pool or nonblocking-throughput claim. Copies preserve existing destination inodes/hard links, follow destination symlinks, compare open source/destination handles before truncation, preserve permissions and enforce exclusive creation. Borrowed descriptors remain open. Reflink flags and nonregular copy remain explicit unsupported operations. Strict `util.promisify` is unchanged.
- The [saved before/after and exact CLI evidence](evidence/2026-11-12-home-callback-fs-mutations.json) executes the unmodified `@napi-rs/cli` **2.18.4** help entry from CI. Both saved Home providers fail before at `promisify original must be a function`; diagnostics identify absent `writeFile`, `unlink`, `copyFile` and `mkdir`. Both after providers genuinely register all six filesystem callbacks and reach missing **`process.binding("constants")`**. Node/Bun help controls pass. No private-binding shim, native addon build/release invocation, or whole-CLI pass was substituted.
- The same 43-group fixture improves **1/43 → 43/43** on both Home providers. Node passes 43/43. Python independently verifies **1,048,593 bytes**, SHA256 `93513902ca696d5666cfebbe008c992048a8c04aec93f5c62b732c165dbc9373`, original hard-link inode identity, symlinks and permissions. All **16** separate success/error/uncaught/fatal/liveness children pass on Node and both Home providers. The full Bun reference passes **21/43 groups** and fails the fixture; its child phase is unexecuted.
